Abstract: Dataset condensation for action segmentation synthesizes compact, informative representations of long, untrimmed video datasets. The existing approach relies on Variational Autoencoders and an iterative latent optimization; it is computationally expensive and suffers from over-smoothed reconstructions and rigid temporal constraints. This paper proposes to shift the condensation paradigm from optimization-based inversion to deterministic latent mapping. By leveraging Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models, we represent action segments as continuous trajectories anchored by sparse latent points in the noise manifold. To maximize representational efficiency, we introduce an adaptive allocation mechanism that dynamically redistributes the anchoring budget based on segment-wise reconstruction difficulty. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in segmentation performance across common datasets. Notably, our approach achieves performance parity with real data training while maintaining a condensation ratio of 2.4\% on Breakfast dataset.

</details>


### [19] [REBASE: Reference-Background Subspace Elimination for Training-Free In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09082)
*Mantha Sai Gopal,Jaison Saji Chacko,Harsh Nandwana,Sandesh Hegde,Debarshi Banerjee,Uma Mahesh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REBASE是一种无需训练的单样本分割框架，通过正交投影去除背景子空间干扰，显著提升跨图像语义匹配质量，在多个数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无需训练的上下文分割方法受限于跨图像相似性图质量——参考图像与查询图像共享的背景上下文会系统性提升非目标区域的相似度，导致提示定位退化。作者旨在显式抑制这些虚假的背景对应关系。

Method: 1) 从参考图像中识别低秩背景特征子空间；2) 将参考和查询特征投影到该子空间的正交补上（闭式解），得到更干净的语义匹配；3) 使用相似性加权的Farthest Point Sampling生成正点提示，并配以精细化的密集相似性先验。

Result: 在PACO-Part、FSS-1000以及跨域数据集ISIC2018上，无需任何训练或参数更新，达到了训练-free方法中的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式去除背景子空间是一种高效的单样本定位原则，为无需训练的上下文分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: Training-free in-context segmentation enables new object categories to be introduced at inference time from a single annotated reference image, eliminating the retraining and memory overhead of class-incremental learning. Recent approaches achieve this by combining vision foundation models for semantic correspondence with promptable segmentation networks like SAM. However, their performance is fundamentally limited by the quality of the cross-image similarity map; shared contextual backgrounds between the reference and query systematically elevate similarity in non-target regions, degrading prompt localization. We present REBASE, a training-free framework that explicitly suppresses these spurious contextual correspondences. Our method identifies the low-rank background feature subspace from the reference image and project the reference and query features onto its orthogonal complement in closed form, yielding cleaner semantic matching. We then generate positive point prompts using similarity-weighted farthest-point sampling, paired with a refined dense similarity prior. Without any training or parameter updates, our approach establishes a new state of the art among training-free methods on PACO-Part, FSS-1000, and cross-domain datasets such as ISIC2018, demonstrating that explicit background subspace removal is a highly effective principle for one-shot localization.

</details>


### [20] [Subtoken Vision Transformer for Fine-grained Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09086)
*Jie Zhu,Ivy Zhang,Minchul Kim,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SubViT是一种选择性图像分词方法，通过用多个子标记表示判别性图像块来提升细粒度视觉识别能力，在广义类别发现任务上将DINOv2的新类别准确率从81.3%提升至84.7%，仅增加0.50ms延迟和3.4%FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Vision Transformer对每个固定大小的图像块仅压缩为一个标记，而细粒度识别任务中判别性差异往往只集中在少数图像块的局部变化上，存在粒度不匹配问题。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：阶段1利用随机注意力头采样子区域微调ViT，使模型接触多样化的子划分模式；阶段2通过特征退化距离识别信息量丰富的注意力图，并将其蒸馏为轻量级单图路由器，直接预测标记重要性分数，无需额外的注意力前向传播。

Result: 在CUB、FGVC-Aircraft和Stanford Cars数据集上，SubViT将DINOv2的平均新类别准确率从81.3%提升至84.7%，仅增加0.50ms延迟和3.4%FLOPs，相比Retina Patch延迟减少73.8%。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上验证了广泛适用性。

Conclusion: SubViT通过选择性子标记分配有效解决了细粒度识别中的粒度不匹配问题，以极小代价显著提升性能，是一种高效且通用的图像分词改进方法。

Abstract: We present Subtoken Vision Transformer (SubViT), a selective image tokenization method for fine-grained visual recognition. Standard Vision Transformers compress each fixed-size patch into a single token, although fine-grained distinctions often depend on localized variations within only a few patches. SubViT addresses this mismatch by representing discriminative patches with multiple subtokens while retaining the original token sequence for global context, thereby allocating additional capacity where it is most needed. Since attention heads encode complementary semantics and extracting attention maps at inference requires an extra backbone forward, we adopt a two-stage training strategy. Stage 1 fine-tunes the ViT using subdivision regions sampled from random attention heads, exposing the model to diverse subdivision patterns. Stage 2 identifies informative attention maps through feature-degradation distances and distills them into a lightweight single-map router, which directly predicts deterministic token-importance scores without a separate attention forward. We evaluate SubViT on Generalized Category Discovery (GCD), a challenging task requiring both fine-grained discrimination and generalization to unlabeled novel categories. Across CUB, FGVC-Aircraft, and Stanford Cars, SubViT improves the average novel-category accuracy of DINOv2 from $81.3\%$ to $84.7\%$, with only $0.50$ ms additional latency and $3.4\%$ more FLOPs, while reducing latency by $73.8\%$ relative to Retina Patch. Results on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 demonstrate its broader applicability.

</details>


### [21] [DETRAM: End-to-end DEtection, Tracking and Recovery of HumAn Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09089)
*Chunggi Lee,Seonwook Park,Wanhua Li,Umar Iqbal,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DETRAM提出了一个统一框架，通过单一Transformer解码器和身份一致的查询嵌入，同时实现多人体网格恢复、检测、跟踪，并支持用户提示跟踪，在多个数据集上达到SOTA跟踪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人物场景中人体网格恢复任务因实体多、遮挡严重而困难，现有方法依赖预训练检测模块，运行慢且限制了跟踪实体数量，缺乏支持用户提示的端到端框架。

Method: 使用单一Transformer解码器，维护跨帧的身份一致可学习查询嵌入：检测查询发现新人、跟踪查询维持已有姿态和形状、提示查询跟踪用户指定身份。

Result: 在PoseTrack21、3DPW、BEDLAM、MuPoTS-3D上达到SOTA跟踪结果，在BEDLAM和3DPW上重建精度具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 首次统一了提示能力和多人体网格恢复与跟踪，形成端到端可训练框架，支持用户导向的视频人体分析。

Abstract: In the task of human mesh recovery (HMR), multi-person scenes are particularly difficult to handle due to the many entities that appear and occlusions between them over time. In particular for video inputs, there is a need to track each entity reliably and consistently. Existing methods rely on pretrained human detection modules, increasing their runtime and limiting the number of tracked entities. We present DETRAM, a unified framework for multi-person HMR and tracking that simultaneously detects, reconstructs, and tracks humans across time, both automatically and via user prompts. DETRAM uses a single transformer decoder with an identity-consistent set of learnable query embeddings that persist across frames: detection queries discover new people, tracking queries maintain pose and shape for existing individuals, and prompt queries follow user-specified identities. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art tracking results on PoseTrack21, 3DPW, BEDLAM, and MuPoTS-3D, and competitive reconstruction accuracy on BEDLAM and 3DPW, while uniquely supporting prompt-based tracking of individuals in multi-person scenes. To our knowledge, this is the first method to unify promptability and multi-person HMR with tracking in an end-to-end trainable framework, enabling user-directed human analysis in videos.

</details>


### [22] [Beyond Time Shifts: Adapting Omni-LLM as a Reference-Free Evaluator for Generative Audio-Visual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09091)
*Yijie Qian,Juncheng Wang,Chao Xu,Huihan Wang,Yuxiang Feng,Yang Liu,Baigui Sun,Yong Liu,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将人类相对感知转化为连续绝对度量的框架，用于评估音视频生成模型中的跨模态同步性，解决了现有指标无法处理生成内容中结构幻觉和不对称跨模态关系的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标假设结构正确性，将同步性简化为时间对齐，无法处理生成内容中的结构幻觉和不对称跨模态关系，依赖人工标注，造成人类相对比较与自动化绝对评估之间的矛盾。

Method: 构建了带有成对人类排名的SynthSync数据集；采用带有连续潜变量投影的Omni-LLM将相对排名转化为连续绝对值；提出实值组相对策略优化（R-GRPO）通过列表级得分分布内化同步的全局因果结构。

Result: 该指标在人类偏好对齐上达到了最先进的性能，并建立了标准化基准，将音视频生成评估从低级信号相关性提升到视觉因果性。

Conclusion: 通过将人类相对感知蒸馏为全局一致的连续度量，成功解决了生成内容中跨模态同步性评估的悖论，推动了音视频生成评估的范式升级。

Abstract: As audio-visual generative models evolve into world simulators, cross-modal synchronization stands as a critical proxy for assessing the consistency of world dynamics and causality in generated content. However, existing evaluation metrics presume structural correctness, reducing synchronization to mere temporal alignment. Consequently, they fail on generative outputs, especially when exhibiting structural hallucinations and asymmetric cross-modal relations, which currently \textbf{mandate expert human annotation to assess synchronization.} This dependency introduces a critical paradox: \emph{human evaluators rely on relative, reference-dependent comparisons, whereas automated metrics require reference-free, absolute scalars.} We resolve this paradox by proposing a framework that distills relative human perception into a continuous, globally consistent metric. First, we introduce SynthSync, a dataset of generative failures ranked via pairwise human annotations. Second, we adapt the Omni-LLM equipped with a continuous latent projection to translate relative human rankings into continuous absolute values. Third, we propose Real-Valued Group Relative Policy Optimization ($\mathbb{R}$-GRPO) to internalize the global causal structure of synchronization via listwise score distributions. Empirically, our metric achieves state-of-the-art human preference alignment. We leverage this estimator to establish a standardized benchmark, advancing AV-Gen assessment from low-level signal correlation to visually grounded causality.

</details>


### [23] [A Coreset Selection Framework with Ensemble Aggregation for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09100)
*Pedro Rocha Dantas,Lucas Pascotti Valem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合coreset选择与集成聚合的框架，其中SCOSS方法通过对训练数据分层采样，在多种分类器和采样率下表现出竞争力，尤其在细粒度数据集上能以更少标注样本超越SVM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像数据快速增长导致模型训练的时间和内存成本高昂，而选择代表性训练子集面临样本贡献不明确和模型行为随数据集与运行变化等挑战。

Method: 提出SCOSS（SCOre-Stratified Selection）方法，基于选定分数将训练数据划分为区间并采样；同时采用集成聚合策略，在多个独立采样子集上运行的预测结果进行组合。

Result: SCOSS在SGC分类器上常为最佳选择，能实现精度与效率的有利权衡；在细粒度数据集上，使用更少标注样本时SGC+SCOSS优于SVM。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效提升了训练子集选择的效率与准确性，SCOSS方法具有竞争力和实用价值。

Abstract: The rapid growth of image data has produced large-scale datasets, raising concerns about the time and memory costs of model training. Selecting representative training subsets, however, remains challenging: individual sample contributions are unclear, and model behavior varies across datasets and runs. We address these challenges with a framework that combines coreset selection with an ensemble aggregation over multiple runs. For coreset selection, we propose SCOre-Stratified Selection (SCOSS), which partitions the training data into intervals based on a chosen score and samples from each interval. The ensemble combines predictions from multiple runs, each performed on an independently sampled training subset. As baselines, we use moderate and random selection, each in original and class-balanced versions. We assess the framework with Simple Graph Convolution (SGC) and Support Vector Machine (SVM) classifiers under different sampling ratios. Experiments show that SCOSS is competitive with baselines, often the best choice for SGC, and enables favorable trade-offs between accuracy and efficiency. On the fine-grained dataset, SGC with SCOSS outperforms SVMs when using fewer labeled samples. The code and supplementary materials are publicly available at http://scoss.lucasvalem.com.

</details>


### [24] [Equivariant Filter for High Performance Image Tracking using an Event Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09103)
*Angus Apps,Yixiao Ge,Timothy L. Molloy,Robert Mahony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件相机的等变滤波器设计，用于高速跟踪平面图像变换，利用AEB跟踪器提取特征位置测量，并通过等变滤波器利用特殊欧几里得群对称性计算仿射变换，实现了每秒7000像素的平滑跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像跟踪在自动驾驶、机器人控制及计算机视觉中至关重要，但传统方法在处理高速运动时面临挑战，需要更高效的设计。

Method: 采用AEB跟踪器从事件流中提取特征位置，结合等变滤波器利用SE(2)群对称性计算仿射变换，并通过等效测量更新步骤去相关高度时间相关的测量数据。

Result: 在涉及一般和快速旋转运动的数据集上，该方法实现了每秒7000像素的平滑跟踪，优于直接优化和协方差交集方法。

Conclusion: 等变滤波器设计有效解决了事件相机高速跟踪中的时序相关性问题，为高速运动场景下的图像跟踪提供了高性能方案。

Abstract: Image tracking is the problem of estimating the transformation that relates a moving image of a scene to an original reference image. The problem is important in control of autonomous vehicles or robots, where the image encodes information about the motion of the camera or environment, as well as in pure computer vision applications. In this paper, we present an equivariant filter design for high performance tracking of planar image transformations using an event camera. The design exploits the Asynchronous Event Blob (AEB) tracker (Wang et al., 2024) to extract feature-position measurements from the raw event stream, and an equivariant filter to compute an affine image translation and rotation using the special Euclidean group symmetry. The equivariant filter incorporates an equivalent-measurement update step that de-correlates the (highly temporally correlated) feature-position measurements provided by the AEB tracker. We evaluate the design experimentally using two datasets involving general and fast rotational motion. We benchmark results against direct optimisation (estimating the relative transformation from the raw blob tracks), and a covariance intersection approach for overcoming data correlation. Our design provides smooth image tracking for features moving up to 7000 pixels per second on the image plane.

</details>


### [25] [Integrating Large Language Models and Graph Convolutional Networks for Semi-Supervised Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09104)
*Camila Piscioneri Magalhães,Lucas Pascotti Valem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）来优化图卷积网络（GCN）中的图结构，通过生成图像文本描述并计算语义相似度来剪枝kNN图中的边，从而提升图像分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像数据标注成本高，半监督学习方法（如GCN）虽有前景，但图像缺乏预定义图结构；现有方法基于特征相似性构建图，但未充分利用高级语义信息，LLM在此领域的应用尚待探索。

Method: 使用VLM生成图像文本描述，再由LLM估计图像间的语义相似度分数，基于该分数对kNN和互kNN图中的边进行剪枝，过滤掉语义不相关的邻居。

Result: 利用LLM进行图优化能提升分类准确率，尤其针对kNN图和部分骨干网络效果显著。

Conclusion: LLM可用于优化GCN的图结构，提高图像分类性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: While the growing availability of image data has driven significant advances, labeling datasets remains costly and time-consuming. Therefore, semi-supervised approaches such as Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs), which learn from both labeled and unlabeled data, have emerged as a promising solution. One of the primary challenges in applying GCNs to image classification is graph construction, since, unlike in citation networks or similar domains, images typically do not come with a predefined structural representation. For visual data, most studies construct graphs based on the similarity between feature vectors from pretrained deep learning backbones, typically by employing kNN or reciprocal kNN algorithms. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capability in capturing high-level semantics, their integration with GCNs for image classification remains underexplored. Aiming to fill this gap, our approach uses a Vision Language Model (VLM) to generate textual image descriptions, which are then processed by an LLM to estimate semantic similarity scores between connected images. These scores guide the pruning of edges in kNN and reciprocal kNN graphs, filtering out semantically irrelevant neighbors. Experimental results reveal that leveraging LLMs for graph refinement can improve classification accuracy, particularly for kNN graphs and some backbones. The source code is publicly available at http://gcnllm.lucasvalem.com.

</details>


### [26] [Event Stream based Multi-Modal Video Anomaly Detection: A Benchmark Dataset and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09114)
*Peipei Zhu,Yueqing Niu,Lin Zhu,Guanchong Niu,Yang Yu,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EVAD框架，结合传统视频与事件相机数据，通过对比多模态预训练和自适应融合模块，解决视频异常检测在光照变化、快速运动等复杂场景下的鲁棒性问题，并构建了包含63亿事件和37.6万帧图像的大规模基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频异常检测在光照变化、快速运动和复杂背景下表现脆弱，仅依赖可见光视频存在局限性。事件相机具有高时间分辨率、对运动模糊和极端光照的鲁棒性等优势，但缺乏多模态VAD的相关数据集和有效方法。

Method: 1) 构建大规模可见光-事件基准数据集（6.3B事件+376,368帧）；2) 设计对比多模态预训练框架，对齐事件流、视频和文本的语义嵌入；3) 提出自适应融合模块，动态整合事件时序特征与视频空间语义。

Result: 在基准数据集和自建TJUTCM Pha数据集上，EVAD方法持续优于现有方法，验证了事件相机在真实场景VAD中的有效性。

Conclusion: 事件增强的视频异常检测框架（EVAD）通过融合事件流与可见光视频，显著提升了复杂环境下的检测鲁棒性，填补了事件相机在异常检测领域的数据和方法空白。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) is critical for automated surveillance but remains fragile under challenging conditions such as illumination variations, fast motion, and complex backgrounds when relying solely on visible light videos. To address these limitations, we propose EVAD, an event enhanced VAD framework that jointly exploits conventional video and event streams captured by bio inspired event cameras. Event sensors asynchronously capture brightness changes with high temporal resolution, offering robustness to motion blur and extreme lighting, and providing motion salient cues complementary to video based visual information. To support multi modal VAD research, we construct a large scale visible event benchmark comprising 6.3 billion events and 376,368 video frames collected under diverse illumination levels, motion patterns, and background complexities, filling the gap of realistic and scalable datasets for event based anomaly detection. Building upon this dataset, we design a contrastive multi modal pretraining framework to learn discriminative event representations by aligning semantic embeddings across event streams, visible videos, and textual descriptions. An adaptive fusion module then dynamically integrates event based temporal cues with video based spatial semantics, improving robustness to environmental disturbances. Experiments on benchmarks and the proposed TJUTCM Pha dataset demonstrate that E VAD consistently outperforms methods, validating the effectiveness of event-based sensing for VAD in real world scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [Event Burst Trigger: An Availability Backdoor Attack on Event-Based SNN Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09115)
*Jaesun Baek,Chanwook Lee,Eun-Kyu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Event Burst Trigger (EBT)的可用性后门攻击，针对基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的事件视觉目标检测模型。EBT通过注入精心设计的触发器，使得推理时产生时间上集中的脉冲流，增加伪目标候选数量，从而显著提升NMS后处理阶段的延迟（最高达38%），而检测精度仅下降不到0.099 mAP@0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视觉和SNN在边缘智能中应用广泛，但针对事件型SNN目标检测模型的可用性后门攻击尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出EBT攻击方法：在训练数据中注入精心设计的事件触发器，使模型在推理时产生时间上集中的脉冲流（burst-like activations），增加伪目标候选数量，从而膨胀NMS阶段的计算开销。该攻击为仅投毒威胁模型，无需修改模型架构、损失函数或推理流程。

Result: 在SpikeYOLO上评估：mAP@0.5下降不超过0.099，但NMS阶段延迟增加高达38%；在边缘平台上，攻击增加了资源利用率并减少了调度余量，且STRIP检测方法无法可靠区分攻击样本与良性输入。

Conclusion: NMS可能成为事件型SNN目标检测系统中的主要可用性瓶颈，揭示了此前未被充分探索的可用性后门威胁。

Abstract: Event-based vision and spiking neural networks (SNNs) are increasingly adopted for edge intelligence under strict latency and energy constraints. However, the vulnerability of event-based SNN object detection models to availability backdoor attacks remains insufficiently studied. This paper presents Event Burst Trigger (EBT), an availability backdoor attack targeting SNN-based object detection models. EBT injects carefully crafted event-based triggers into the training data, which induce temporally concentrated event streams during inference. These burst-like activations increase the number of phantom (i.e., spurious) object candidates, and consequently inflate the computational cost of the post-processing stage, particularly Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS). We evaluate EBT on SpikeYOLO, the state-of-the-art SNN-based object detector, under a poison-only threat model that does not require modifications to the model architecture, loss function, or inference pipeline. Experimental results show that while detection accuracy remains largely preserved, with mAP@0.5 decreasing by less than 0.099, the latency of the NMS stage increases by up to 38%. This indicates that NMS can become a dominant availability bottleneck in event-based SNN object detection. Experiments on an edge platform further show that the proposed attack elevates baseline resource utilization and reduces scheduling slack without inducing conspicuous peaks in resource usage. In addition, STRIP-based backdoor detection fails to reliably distinguish the proposed attack from benign inputs. These results characterize a previously underexplored availability backdoor threat in event-based SNN object detection systems.

</details>


### [28] [4D Human-Scene Reconstruction from Low-Overlap Captures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09125)
*Minhyuk Hwang,Sangmin Kim,Seunguk Do,Daneul Kim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StudioRecon提出了一种从稀疏、低重叠摄像头重建4D人体场景的流水线，通过解耦背景与人体、扩散模型合成新视角、交叉视图身份关联等策略，实现了最先进的新视角合成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态人体体积捕获依赖密集摄像头阵列，现实场景中仅有少量低重叠摄像头，导致输出质量下降且存在大量未观察区域；现有4D重建方法在低重叠场景仍有明显伪影，视频扩散模型则存在几何不一致问题。

Method: 1) 解耦背景与人体；2) 利用视频扩散模型合成数百个受相机控制的新视角以稠化背景监督；3) 通过交叉视图身份关联和三角化多视图关键点拟合鲁棒初始化可变形高斯人体；4) 递归增强模块结合运动自适应一致性注入，消除残留伪影。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上实现了最先进的新视角合成效果，并展示了新颖轨迹渲染和人体替换等应用。

Conclusion: StudioRecon通过解耦策略与扩散模型结合，有效解决了稀疏低重叠摄像头下4D人体重建的难题，在多个数据集上达到最优性能。

Abstract: Existing volumetric capture of dynamic human performance achieves high fidelity with dense camera arrays. However, in real-world scenarios, only a handful of low-overlap cameras are available, which degrades the output quality and leaves large areas unobserved. Recent 4D reconstruction methods have focused on low-overlap settings, yet they still produce noticeable artifacts in under-observed regions. Video diffusion models have emerged as another option, but they show geometrically inconsistent results for humans. To address these limitations, we propose StudioRecon, a pipeline that reconstructs 4D human scenes from sparse, low-overlap cameras by decoupling background and humans. We densify background supervision by synthesizing hundreds of camera-controlled novel views with a video diffusion model. We also robustly initialize deformable Gaussian humans with cross-view identity association and triangulated multi-view keypoint fitting. Finally, our recursive enhancement module with motion-adaptive consistency injection harmonizes the composed output, thereby further avoiding remaining artifacts. We achieve state-of-the-art novel view synthesis across four real-world datasets and demonstrate applications such as novel trajectory rendering and human replacement.

</details>


### [29] [VTaMo: Video-Text Alignment Model for Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09126)
*Junyi Hu,Zhewen He,Haomian Huang,Aoxiang Yang,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTaMo是一种手语翻译框架，通过三层次显式对齐（局部最优传输、全局正交变换、位置对比学习）提升跨模态语义匹配，在四个数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无词汇表（gloss-free）的手语翻译方法依赖预训练编码器和语言模型，仅通过翻译损失隐式学习跨模态对齐，缺乏显式的细粒度对齐机制，导致视觉与文本语义匹配不充分。

Method: 提出VTaMo框架，包含三个层次的对齐：(1)局部对齐：基于熵正则化最优传输，引入可学习的空标记（null token）实现帧到词元的细粒度对应；(2)全局对齐：通过可学习正交变换，利用推土机距离（Earth Mover's Distance）校准嵌入空间几何；(3)位置对齐对比学习：提升词元级别表示的判别性。

Result: 在Phoenix-2014T、CSL-Daily、How2Sign和OpenASL四个数据集上均取得一致的最优性能，消融实验验证了各组件的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 显式的多粒度对齐能够有效提升无词汇表手语翻译的跨模态语义匹配能力，所提出的三层次对齐策略互补有效，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) converts continuous sign videos into spoken language text. Gloss-free approaches leverage pre-trained visual encoders and language models but rely on implicit cross-modal alignment from translation supervision alone. We present VTaMo, a framework that introduces explicit multi-granularity alignment at three levels: (1) local alignment via entropy-regularized optimal transport with a learnable null token for fine-grained frame-to-token correspondences; (2) global alignment via a learnable orthogonal transformation that calibrates embedding space geometry through Earth Mover's Distance; and (3) position-aligned contrastive learning for discriminative token-level representations. Experiments on Phoenix-2014T, CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and OpenASL demonstrate consistent state-of-the-art performance, with ablations confirming the complementary contributions of each component. Code is available at https://github.com/junyi2005/vtamo.

</details>


### [30] [IB-Flow: Information Bottleneck-Guided CFG Distillation for Few-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09133)
*Yiting Wang,Jingyi Zhang,Wenhu Zhang,Ke Chao,Yves Liang,Kun Cheng,Kang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息瓶颈理论的双轨自适应蒸馏框架，通过实例感知的注入目标选择和熵感知的注入强度调度，解决了少步扩散模型中CFG过度条件化伪影问题，在2步推理配置下达到了SOTA生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步蒸馏方法采用全局静态引导强度的盲目注入范式，忽视了图像生成作为动态演化过程的本质（渐进熵减），导致CFG过度条件化伪影，限制了少步压缩的性能边界。

Method: 将蒸馏过程建模为受信息瓶颈约束的动态互信息博弈，提出双轨自适应框架：(1) 实例感知选择机制，基于局部向量场范数将KL散度约束转化为零开销闭式解；(2) 熵感知调度策略，沿信噪比动态衰减引导强度，初始提供最大结构锚定力，后期平滑回归自然流形细化微观细节。

Result: 实验证明该方法从根本上消除了过度条件化伪影，在极严格的2步推理配置下打破了性能天花板，达到了SOTA生成保真度。

Conclusion: 状态感知的动态蒸馏策略比传统盲注入范式更有效，信息瓶颈理论为理解少步蒸馏提供了新视角。

Abstract: While large-scale text-to-image generative models have achieved unprecedented visual performance, their inherent reliance on multi-step iterative solvers incurs severe inference latency. Few-step distillation targeting the Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) trajectory has emerged as the prevalent dual-dimensional compression paradigm. However, existing frameworks remain subjugated by a coarse-grained blind injection paradigm that perpetually enforces a globally static guidance strength while indiscriminately sampling the supervisor timestep. This state-agnostic design completely disregards the intrinsic nature of image generation as a dynamic evolutionary process characterized by progressive entropy reduction, which not only restricts the performance boundary of few-step compression but also precipitates severe CFG over-conditioning artifacts. To transcend these limitations, we re-examine the distillation procedure through the theoretical lens of Information Theory, formally modeling it as a dynamic mutual information game constrained by the Information Bottleneck (IB) principle. Specifically, we dismantle traditional blind assumptions via a dual-track adaptive framework. To determine the injection target, we propose an instance-aware selection mechanism that transmutes the intractable KL divergence constraint into a zero-overhead closed-form solution predicated on the local vector field norm. To regulate the injection strength, we introduce an entropy-aware schedule that dynamically decays alongside the SNR, applying maximal thrust for initial structural anchoring before smoothly reverting to the natural manifold to refine micro-details. Extensive empirical evaluations corroborate that our framework fundamentally eradicates over-conditioning artifacts, shattering the performance ceiling to achieve SOTA generative fidelity under extremely stringent 2-step configurations.

</details>


### [31] [Super-Generalist: Towards Comprehensive and Accurate Medical Image Understanding via Generalist-Specialist Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09135)
*Shaoteng Zhang,Weiwei Cao,Wanxing Chang,Yutong Xie,Kai Cao,Zaiyi Liu,Yu Shi,Tingbo Liang,Qi Zhang,Ling Zhang,Yong Xia,Jianpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SuG是一个统一通用视觉语言学习与专家目标的超级通用框架，通过融合多分割专家的空间先验，在胸部与腹部CT诊断任务上实现了超越通用模型和专家模型的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言通用模型缺乏细粒度解剖与病灶感知能力，而监督专家模型虽性能强但泛化性差，难以兼顾广泛任务覆盖与精准诊断。

Method: 提出Super-Generalist（SuG）框架，融合通用视觉语言学习与专家目标；利用多种分割专家（解剖、病灶）提供空间先验，通过病灶掩码校准文本条件视觉注意力，增强病灶定位能力。

Result: 在CT-RATE、Merlin、MedVL-CT69K及多个内部肿瘤数据集上达到SOTA，在多个关键肿瘤诊断基准上超越专家模型，并展现出强病灶定位与泛化能力。

Conclusion: SuG成功统一了通用模型与专家模型的优势，验证了空间先验与视觉语言对齐结合的有效性，为医学影像诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Medical images require comprehensive and accurate interpretation to support the diagnosis of diverse clincial conditions. Recent vision-language generalist models offer broad task coverage and promising zero-shot capabilities, yet often lack fine-grained anatomical and lesion awareness for reliable diagnosis and spatial interpretability. In contrast, supervised specialist models achieve strong performance on specific tasks but typically lack generalization across diseases and anatomies. In this work, we present SuG, a Super-Generalist framework that unifies generalist vision-language learning with specialist objectives, enabling both broad generalization and specialist-level diagnostic capability. We perform specialist-enhanced vision-language alignment in SuG by incorporating spatial priors from multiple segmentation experts, including anatomy, class-specific lesion and class-agnostic lesion segmentors that captures lesions beyond anatomies annotated during training. To improve lesion grounding capability, we leverage lesion masks as spatial priors to calibrate text-conditioned visual attention, encouraging disease-related semantics to focus on clinically relevant regions. We evaluate SuG on extensive chest and abdominal CT benchmarks, including CT-RATE, Merlin, MedVL-CT69K, and several in-house tumor datasets. SuG achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of disease diagnosis tasks and surpasses specialist models on several critical tumor diagnosis benchmarks. Furthermore, SuG demonstrates strong lesion grounding capability, including robust generalization to lesion types lacking class-specific supervision.

</details>


### [32] [Weaving Light and Time: Unified Harmonic-Geometric Representation Learning for Dense RGB-Event Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09143)
*Chenxu Peng,Chongtian zhou,Dicheng Liu,Bo-Wen Yin,Yimian Dai,Xialei Liu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evita是首个专门为密集RGB-事件联合解析设计的统一骨干网络，通过内嵌几何视差校正、谐波光谱共振和瞬态全局路由等共学习模块，并配合N-ImageNetV2数据集与随机事件表示混合预训练策略，在多个基准上实现了SOTA性能与更优的精度-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-事件融合方法存在两大瓶颈：解耦双编码器架构计算开销翻倍，而通用统一设计无法解决密集强度图像与稀疏运动事件之间的几何视差和跨光谱混叠问题。

Method: 提出Evita统一骨干网络，在每个编码层嵌入三个共学习模块：几何视差校正（自适应空间对齐）、谐波光谱共振（复数频域纹理迁移）、瞬态全局路由（事件驱动的非对称注意力）。同时构建N-ImageNetV2数据集和随机事件表示混合预训练协议，解耦特征与特定事件编码格式。

Result: 在DELIVER、DDD17和DSEC基准上取得新的SOTA指标，同时实现了更优的精度-延迟权衡，适用于实时多模态感知。

Conclusion: Evita是首个专为密集RGB-事件解析设计的统一骨干网络，通过内嵌的共学习模块和创新的预训练策略，有效解决了模态融合中的几何视差与光谱混叠问题，在性能与效率上均超越现有方法。

Abstract: Fusing standard RGB frames with asynchronous event streams has emerged as a definitive paradigm for robust perception in degraded environments. Although unified backbones have recently gained traction in multi-modal vision, adapting them to the RGB-Event domain remains fundamentally challenging. Existing architectures either resort to decoupled dual encoders that double computational overhead, or adopt generic unified designs that fail to resolve implicit geometric parallax and cross-spectral aliasing under the extreme representational divide between dense intensity grids and sparse kinematic spikes. To transcend these bottlenecks, we present Evita, the first unified backbone specifically engineered for dedicated dense RGB-Event parsing. To achieve profound modal synergy, Evita explicitly embeds a suite of intrinsic co-learning modules directly into every encoder layer. Specifically, it features Geometric Parallax Rectification for adaptive spatial alignment, Harmonic Spectral Resonance for texture transfer exclusively in the complex frequency domain, and Transient Global Routing for event-driven asymmetric attention. To guarantee robust feature extraction against spatial misalignments and decouple representations from specific event encodings, we construct N-ImageNetV2 alongside a stochastic event representation mixing pretraining protocol, empowering the network to seamlessly accommodate arbitrary event formats in downstream tasks. Extensive evaluations across the DELIVER, DDD17, and DSEC benchmarks confirm that Evita establishes new state-of-the-art metrics while delivering a superior accuracy-latency trade-off for real-time multimodal perception.The code are publicly available at: https://github.com/chaineypung/Evita.

</details>


### [33] [What Pixels Are Enough? SEAMS: Sufficiency Saliency via MSE-Preservation Soft-Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09164)
*Magdalena Trędowicz,Łukasz Struski,Arkadiusz Lewicki,Karolina Pachota,Andrzej Grudzień,Mateusz Jagła,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAMS是一种基于充分性的显著性方法，通过优化软掩码来保留模型行为，无需辅助数据集或架构特定机制，可生成对象级、类别条件和Token级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性方法往往需要辅助数据集、架构特定机制或可微top-k松弛，SEAMS旨在以统一简洁的优化框架解决这些问题。

Method: 基于软掩码和可学习预算的优化框架，通过三路图像复合和保留目标（如类别概率、CLS嵌入、Token表示）来搜索紧凑掩码，无需辅助数据集或架构特定归因机制。

Result: 在冻结的ViT-S/16和ConvNeXt模型上，SEAMS生成的掩码紧凑、可解释、对随机初始化稳定，在插入和删除基准测试中具有竞争力。不同架构在相似保真度下依赖不同充分证据。

Conclusion: SEAMS提供了一种统一、简洁且有效的充分性显著性方法，并揭示了视觉解释的架构依赖性。

Abstract: Saliency maps are most useful when they identify the image regions that are sufficient to preserve a model's behaviour. We introduce SEAMS, a sufficiency-based saliency method that directly optimises a soft mask using a preservation objective. Given a frozen differentiable model output, such as a class probability, CLS embedding, or token representation, SEAMS searches for a compact mask that preserves the selected output. The approach relies on a simple optimisation framework based on soft masks, a learnable budget, and a three-way image composite generated entirely from the query image. As a result, it requires no auxiliary distractor dataset, architecture-specific attribution mechanism, or differentiable top-k relaxation. Experiments with frozen ViT-S/16 and ConvNeXt models show that the same optimisation pipeline can generate object-level, class-conditioned, and token-level explanations by changing only the preserved target. The resulting masks are compact, interpretable, stable across random initialisations, and competitive on insertion and deletion benchmarks. Our results also indicate that different architectures often rely on different sufficient evidence while achieving similar preservation fidelity, highlighting the architecture-dependent nature of visual explanations.

</details>


### [34] [TSR-Ego: Temporally Guided Stereo Refinement Framework for Egocentric 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09169)
*Md Mushfiqur Azam,John Quarles,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TSR-Ego，一种时序引导的立体框架，通过因果深度可分离时序卷积和因果立体解码器，利用运动上下文增强立体特征和关节表示，在UnrealEgo2和UnrealEgo-RW上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖当前帧的局部线索或仅将时序信息作为辅助姿态级别的上下文，在立体线索弱、遮挡或模糊时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 1) 因果深度可分离时序卷积丰富立体特征图；2) 单阶段因果立体解码器包含时序自注意力、关节自注意力和鱼眼可变形立体交叉注意力，利用姿态估计生成2D采样参考。

Result: 在UnrealEgo2和UnrealEgo-RW数据集上达到SOTA性能，尤其在真实世界序列上有显著提升。

Conclusion: TSR-Ego通过将运动上下文融入特征采样和关节表示，有效提升了弱立体线索下的鲁棒性，且支持在线推理。

Abstract: Egocentric 3D human pose estimation from head-mounted stereo cameras is challenging due to fisheye distortion, severe self-occlusion, and frequent truncation of body joints outside the camera field of view. Recent stereo egocentric methods have improved performance through heatmap lifting, stereo correspondence, and transformer-based refinement, but they often rely heavily on frame-local evidence or use temporal information only as auxiliary pose-level context. This limits robustness when current-frame stereo cues are weak, occluded, or ambiguous. We propose TSR-Ego, a temporally guided stereo framework that couples short-term motion evidence with projection-guided feature sampling. The model first enriches dense stereo feature maps using a causal depthwise-separable temporal convolution, allowing past visual evidence to influence the feature space before deformable cross-attention. A single-stage causal stereo decoder then refines learned 3D joint queries through temporal self-attention, joint self-attention, and fisheye deformable stereo cross-attention, using the evolving pose estimate to generate 2D sampling references. Unlike methods that apply temporal reasoning mainly after pose prediction, TSR-Ego uses motion context to shape both the sampled stereo features and the joint representations while preserving online inference without future frames. Experiments on UnrealEgo2 and UnrealEgo-RW show state-of-the-art performance, with especially strong gains on real-world sequences.

</details>


### [35] [Causally Debiased Latent Action Model for Embodied Action Conditioned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09185)
*Yufan Wei,Kun Zhou,Lingjun Mao,Zijun Zhang,Ziming Xu,Ziqiao Xi,Shuang Liang,Ruobing Han,Yuchen Yan,Xinyue Wang,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CD-LAM框架，通过因果去偏方法解决潜动作模型中的动作无关偏置问题，使基于潜动作的条件世界模型具备更好的可控性，仅需少量微调即可显著提升机器人动作跟随性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于潜动作的条件世界模型（ACWMs）训练成本高，需要大规模带动作标签的数据；而潜动作模型（LAMs）虽可用无标签视频学习，但存在动作无关偏置（如背景、未触碰物体等），导致动作可控性差。

Method: 提出CD-LAM框架，包含三种高效的微调目标：以具身为中心的重建、以动作为中心的对比学习、以及潜空间校准，共同鼓励具身聚焦、动作感知且校准的非坍缩潜动作表示。

Result: 在2B和14B规模的ACWM骨干网络上，CD-LAM显著提升了潜动作可控性、下游机器人动作跟随能力、视觉保真度和适应效率，仅需6K步微调，且机器人动作适应更新次数比基线减少12倍以上。

Conclusion: 提出的CD-LAM框架有效解决了LAM中的动作无关偏置问题，为低成本、高效率的机器人世界模型学习提供了可行思路。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) aim to simulate future observations conditioned on embodied actions, offering a promising foundation for robot planning, policy evaluation, and data augmentation. However, learning controllable ACWMs requires large-scale action-labeled data, which remains costly to collect in the real world. Latent action models (LAMs) mitigate this bottleneck by inferring latent actions from unlabeled videos, but existing LAMs are typically trained with reconstruction-only objectives and therefore entangle action-relevant dynamics with action-irrelevant visual factors such as backgrounds and untouched objects. In this work, we identify this action-irrelevant bias as a key obstacle to controllable ACWMs and introduce evaluation metrics to measure latent-action bias, action following, and robustness. We propose CD-LAM, a causally debiased framework for LAM-based ACWMs. CD-LAM introduces three efficient fine-tuning objectives: embodiment-centric reconstruction, action-centric contrastive learning, and latent space calibration, which together encourage embodiment-focused, action-aware, and calibrated non-collapsed latent action representations. Experiments on 2B and 14B ACWM backbones show that CD-LAM substantially improves latent-action controllability, downstream robot-action following, visual fidelity, and adaptation efficiency, requiring only 6k fine-tuning steps and more than 12$\times$ fewer robot-action adaptation updates than the baseline.

</details>


### [36] [HiHR: Hierarchical Hyperbolic Representation for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09186)
*Qiwei Yang,Pingping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种层次化双曲表示（HiHR）框架，用于解决空中-地面行人重识别（AG-ReID）中跨视角特征对齐忽略视角特有信息的问题。通过文本引导的多粒度融合和层次化双曲学习，有效聚合了视角不变和视角特有的判别特征，在四个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AG-ReID方法在跨视角特征对齐时忽略了视角特有的判别线索，导致性能次优。

Method: 1) 基于预训练视觉-文本编码器提取多粒度特征；2) 文本引导的多粒度融合（TMF）增强身份特征表示；3) 层次化双曲学习（HHL）在双曲空间中构建层次特征结构，粗粒度保证身份可分性和跨视角一致性，细粒度保留视角特有判别信息。

Result: 在四个AG-ReID基准数据集上进行了大量实验，证明了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: HiHR框架能够有效聚合视角不变和视角特有的判别特征，提升AG-ReID性能。

Abstract: Aerial-Ground Person Re-IDentification (AG-ReID) aims to retrieve the same person across heterogeneous aerial and ground camera platforms. Although great progress has been made, existing methods remain suboptimal due to the direct feature alignment across views, overlooking view-specific cues. To address this issue, we propose a novel Hierarchical Hyperbolic Representation (HiHR) framework for AG-ReID. More specifically, we first extract multi-granularity features based on pre-trained visual-text encoders. Then, we propose a Text-guided Multi-granularity Fusion (TMF) to fuse multi-granularity features and enhance the representation ability of identity features. Furthermore, we introduce the Hierarchical Hyperbolic Learning (HHL) to construct a hierarchical feature structure in a hyperbolic space. This hierarchy includes a coarse level that ensures identity separability and cross-view consistency, and a fine level that preserves view-specific discriminative cues. As a result, our proposed framework can effectively aggregate view-invariant and view-specific discriminative features for AG-ReID. Extensive experiments on four AG-ReID benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. The source code is available at https://github.com/YangQiWei3/HiHR.

</details>


### [37] [YeTI: You Only Need Two Noisy Images for Real-World sRGB Noise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09193)
*Jaekyun Ko,Byung Wan Lim,Soomin Lee,Dongjin Kim,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YeTI是一种仅需同一场景的两张带噪图像即可进行真实sRGB噪声生成的方法，无需干净图像或相机元数据，通过重建自编码器和一致性扩散Transformer实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实sRGB图像去噪面临传感器噪声非线性特性以及难以获取对齐的干净-带噪图像对的挑战。有监督方法容易过拟合有限配对数据，自监督方法仍依赖足够多样的噪声观测，因此需要一种无需干净真值或相机元数据即可建模真实噪声的可扩展噪声合成方法。

Method: YeTI使用重建自编码器（Reconstruction Autoencoder）解耦场景结构与噪声特征，并通过基于一致性目标训练的单步条件扩散Transformer（Conditional Diffusion Transformer）来建模潜在噪声分布。推理时仅需单张带噪输入即可生成真实、信号相关的噪声。

Result: 在SIDD、SIDD+、MAI2021和SID等真实世界基准上验证了噪声生成效果，涵盖智能手机和多种消费相机传感器。下游去噪实验表明，使用YeTI合成图像训练的去噪器在DND基准上取得了强真实世界性能。

Conclusion: YeTI实现了无需干净图像和相机元数据的真实噪声生成，为可扩展的真实世界去噪提供了实用价值。

Abstract: Real-world sRGB image denoising remains challenging due to the nonlinear characteristics of sensor noise and the difficulty of acquiring aligned clean-noisy image pairs. Supervised denoisers often overfit to limited paired datasets, while self-supervised methods still depend on sufficiently diverse noisy observations. These limitations motivate scalable noise synthesis methods that can model real-world noise without clean ground truth or camera metadata. We propose YeTI, a real-world sRGB noise generation framework that learns from only two noisy observations of the same scene. YeTI uses a Reconstruction Autoencoder to disentangle scene structure and noise characteristics, and models the latent noise distribution with a one-step Conditional Diffusion Transformer trained using consistency objectives. Given a single noisy input at inference time, YeTI generates realistic, signal-dependent noise while preserving the underlying scene content. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of YeTI across real-world benchmarks. We evaluate noise generation on SIDD and further assess generalization on SIDD+, MAI2021, and SID, covering smartphone and diverse consumer-camera sensors. Downstream denoising results on DND further show that denoisers trained with YeTI-synthesized images achieve strong real-world performance, highlighting the practical value of clean-image-free and metadata-free noise generation.

</details>


### [38] [Glob3R: Global Structure-from-Motion with 3D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09225)
*Junyuan Deng,Heng Li,Kejie Qiu,Lingteng Qiu,Rui Peng,Weichao Shen,Weihao Yuan,Siyu Zhu,Zilong Dong,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Glob3R是一种基于3D基础模型的全局SfM风格重建方法，通过显式优化前馈几何预测（密集匹配头、滑动窗口关联、全局运动平均与光束法平差），在室内外、大规模驾驶和SfM基准上实现了比现有前馈模型和经典SfM更鲁棒、更精准的3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D几何基础模型（如VGGT）虽能前馈式预测相机姿态和场景点，但结果不精确，且扩展到长序列或大规模无序图像集时需分块处理，导致漂移和不一致性问题。

Method: 1) 在冻结的Pi3X骨干网络上附加轻量级密集匹配头，预测参考帧与邻域视图间的图像变形，并转换为稀疏但可靠的多视图特征轨迹；2) 引入基于关键帧的滑动窗口关联策略，跨重叠窗口传播轨迹和相对姿态；3) 执行全局运动平均和光束法平差，优化相机姿态、消除尺度不一致，恢复密集场景几何。

Result: 在室内、室外、大规模驾驶以及无序SfM基准测试中，Glob3R实现了鲁棒且精确的重建，持续优于前馈基础模型基线和最新的可扩展重建方法，且比经典SfM流水线更鲁棒。精化后的姿态还带来了更高质量的神经渲染。

Conclusion: 将基础模型的先验知识与全局几何优化相结合，能够有效弥补前馈预测的不足，实现可扩展、鲁棒且高精度的3D重建，同时提升下游任务（如神经渲染）的质量。

Abstract: Recent 3D geometric foundation models, such as VGGT, provide robust feed-forward 3D reconstruction by directly predicting camera poses and 3D scene points from input images. However, their results remain inaccurate, and scaling them to long sequences or large unordered image sets typically requires chunk-wise processing, which can introduce drift and inconsistency. We present Glob3R, a global SfM-style reconstruction built on 3D foundation models. Our key idea is to explicitly optimize feed-forward geometric predictions. To this end, we augment a frozen Pi3X backbone with a lightweight dense matching head that predicts image warps between selected reference frames and neighboring views. These dense warps are converted into sparse but reliable multi-view feature tracks, which provide correspondence constraints for global optimization. We further introduce a keyframe-based sliding-window association strategy that propagates tracks and relative poses across overlapping windows, enabling scalable reconstruction. Finally, we perform global motion averaging and bundle adjustment to refine camera poses, reduce scale inconsistencies, and recover dense scene geometry. Extensive experiments on indoor, outdoor, large-scale driving, and unordered SfM benchmarks demonstrate that Glob3R achieves robust and accurate reconstruction. It consistently improves over feed-forward foundation-model baselines and recent scalable reconstruction methods, while being more robust than classical SfM pipelines. The refined poses also lead to higher-quality neural rendering, validating the benefit of combining foundation-model priors with global geometric optimization. Project page: https://junyuandeng.github.io/Glob3r

</details>


### [39] [AnythingReality: Robust Online Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Open-Vocabulary VR Scene Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09260)
*Timofei Kozlov,Dmitrii Maliukov,Andrey Marchenko,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成ORB-SLAM3位姿估计、在线3D高斯泼溅重建、VR实时探索和语音驱动VLM交互的架构，在噪声真实数据上显著提升了图像质量和物体识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线高斯泼溅方法依赖干净的深度图或外部位姿，难以应对真实世界中带噪声的数据，且缺乏沉浸式交互能力。

Method: 集成ORB-SLAM3进行鲁棒位姿估计，结合在线高斯重建；搭建VR管线实现增量式重建的沉浸式探索；语义模块实现语音转录、场景描述生成和兴趣点记录。

Result: 在自有数据集上PSNR提升14.5%、SSIM提升8.6%、LPIPS降低14.3%；在TUM-RGBD上PSNR提升11.7%、SSIM提升7.8%、LPIPS降低21.6%；VLM物体识别率达到88%。

Conclusion: 该集成架构在噪声真实数据上性能优于现有方法，同时实现了VR交互和语音驱动的VLM语义理解，具备实用性。

Abstract: We present a novel integrated architecture for robust online 3D Gaussian splatting, real-time VR exploration, and speech-driven Vision-Language-Model interaction. Unlike methods assuming clean depth or external poses, our system combines ORB-SLAM3-based pose estimation with online Gaussian reconstruction for noisy real-world data. A VR pipeline enables immersive exploration of incremental reconstructions; a semantic module transcribes voice commands, generates scene descriptions, and records points of interest. Against state-of-the-art online Gaussian splatting methods, we improve image quality on our dataset (+14.5% PSNR, +8.6% SSIM, -14.3% LPIPS) and TUM-RGBD (+11.7% PSNR, +7.8% SSIM, -21.6% LPIPS), with comparable or superior frame rates via quality-speed configurations. We achieve an 88% VLM object-recognition rate.

</details>


### [40] [Semantic Hardness Is Not Visual Hardness: Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining for Sign Language Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09263)
*Junmyeong Lee,Chan Hur,ChangSu Choi,Sukmin Cho,Fitsum Gaim,Eui Jun Hwang,Hoyun Song,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对手语检索中细粒度场景表现脆弱的问题，本文提出基于视觉混淆度构建难负样本的Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining (SAN)方法，有效缓解了负样本分布不匹配问题，在PHOENIX-2014T数据集上显著提升了细粒度检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语检索模型在细粒度场景（需要区分视觉相似但语义不同的手势）中表现脆弱，原因是负样本分布不匹配：语义不同但视觉混淆的手势很少被作为难负样本，而基于文本的挖掘策略无法捕捉这种视觉歧义性。

Method: 提出Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining (SAN)方法，在手语嵌入空间中基于视觉混淆度（而非语言相似性）构建难负样本，从而更好地对齐负样本监督与视觉歧义性。

Result: 在PHOENIX-2014T数据集上，SAN显著提升了细粒度检索性能，同时保持了粗粒度检索的准确性。

Conclusion: 在手语检索中，负样本监督应与视觉歧义性对齐，这一思路对于提升细粒度检索性能至关重要。

Abstract: Sign Language Retrieval (SLRet) enables efficient access to sign language content but remains fragile in fine-grained scenarios where visually similar signs must be distinguished. We show that this limitation does not stem from model capacity, but from ineffective hard negative supervision. Specifically, we formulate fine-grained retrieval failures as a negative distribution mismatch: semantically distinct yet visually confusable signs are rarely treated as hard negatives, while existing text-based mining strategies fail to capture such visual ambiguity. To address this issue, we propose Sign-Aware Hard Negative Mining (SAN), which constructs hard negatives based on visual confusability in the sign embedding space rather than linguistic similarity. Experiments on PHOENIX-2014T demonstrate that SAN substantially improves fine-grained retrieval performance while preserving coarse-grained accuracy, highlighting the importance of aligning negative supervision with visual ambiguity in sign language retrieval.

</details>


### [41] [REMIND: RE-Identification with Memory for INDoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09267)
*Pablo Diaz-Pereda,Alejandro Rodriguez-Ramos,David Perez-Saura,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出REMIND，一种面向室内场景的通用物体长期多目标重识别在线追踪器。它结合冻结的DINOv3特征、双库多原型外观记忆、部位与背景级别描述符、邻域上下文推理模块和联合匈牙利分配算法，在自制数据集上达到90.35% IDF1，比SOTA视频目标分割基线高出近20个百分点，在ScanNet++上几乎在所有设置中取得最高IDF1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法满足室内机器人对通用物体进行长期重识别的需求：多目标追踪方法优化的是短期行人/车辆关联，行人/车辆重识别缺乏持久记忆机制，视频目标分割依赖反应性干扰过滤而非全局身份一致性。

Method: REMIND结合冻结的DINOv3特征、双库多原型外观记忆（dual-bank multi-prototype appearance memory）、部位与背景级别描述符、邻域上下文推理模块（利用空间共现关系）以及联合匈牙利分配与歧义感知安全机制。

Result: 在自制室内数据集上达到90.35% IDF1，比SOTA视频目标分割基线提升近20个百分点，比强追踪-检测基线提升超过36个百分点。在ScanNet++上几乎在所有设置中取得最高IDF1，且能完成所有场景，而对比基线在66.9%的场景下因GPU内存耗尽而失败。

Conclusion: REMIND在无需相机位姿或深度信息的情况下，仅凭单目RGB图像即可实现室内通用物体的长期多目标重识别，效果显著优于现有基线，且完整系统、评估框架和数据集均已开源。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating indoors must re-identify previously observed objects after long temporal gaps, significant viewpoint changes, and severe illumination variations. This remains a challenging problem: multi-object tracking methods are optimized for short-term association of pedestrians and vehicles at video rates, person and vehicle re-identification approaches lack persistent memory mechanisms, and state-of-the-art video object segmentation techniques rely on reactive distractor filtering rather than enforcing global identity consistency.
  To address these limitations, we present REMIND, an online tracker designed for long-term multi-object re-identification of generic indoor objects from monocular RGB imagery, requiring neither camera pose nor depth. Motivated by evidence from visual cognition that humans rely on accumulated appearance familiarity and spatial context rather than explicit self-localization, REMIND combines frozen DINOv3 features with a dual-bank multi-prototype appearance memory, part- and background-level descriptors, a neighbour-context reasoning module exploiting spatial co-occurrence, and joint Hungarian assignment with ambiguity-aware safeguards. On a purpose-built indoor dataset featuring controlled revisits and dense same-class clutter, REMIND reaches 90.35% IDF1, nearly 20 points above a state-of-the-art video object segmentation baseline and more than 36 above a strong tracking-by-detection baseline. On ScanNet++, it attains the highest IDF1 in every setting but one, end-to-end detection over all scenes, where the tracking-by-detection baseline is marginally ahead while REMIND still associates and recovers identities more accurately; it also completes every scene, whereas the video object segmentation baseline exhausts GPU memory on 66.9% under YOLO detections. The complete system, evaluation framework, and dataset are publicly released.

</details>


### [42] [Rethinking Monocular Depth Embedding for Generalized Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09284)
*Libo Lin,Shuangli Du,Minghua Zhao,Zhenzhen You,Shun Lv,Yiguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的单目深度嵌入方法，通过减少分支耦合、构建软约束、融合单目深度特征到特征提取和GRU迭代中，并引入边缘感知损失，在多个立体匹配基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目方法缺乏几何精度，立体方法在无纹理和遮挡区域表现不佳，而两者结合时存在对齐困难且不可靠的单目线索会降低性能。

Method: 1) 减少分支耦合防止捷径学习；2) 构建软约束替代硬约束提高对单目深度误差的容忍度；3) 将单目深度图与RGB融合增强深度边界感知；4) 利用单目深度梯度特征指导视差更新；5) 提出边缘置信度估计和边缘感知损失函数。

Result: 在多个标准基准上达到SOTA性能，泛化能力优异，准确性提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的单目深度嵌入策略有效结合了单目和立体方法的优势，在提升泛化能力的同时提高了匹配精度。

Abstract: Generally, monocular methods capture rich contextual priors but lack geometric precision, whereas stereo methods are geometrically accurate yet struggle in textureless and occluded regions. Several approaches attempt to combine their strengths to enhance the generalization of stereo matching (SM) by aligning monocular depth with stereo information. However, establishing a stable and generalizable alignment is challenging, and unreliable monocular cues can substantially degrade performance. This paper rethinks monocular depth embedding. First, to prevent shortcut learning, we reduce branch coupling instead of expanding network width. Second, we construct soft constraints instead of hard ones from monocular depth to improve tolerance to monocular depth errors. Based on the principles, we integrate monocular information into both feature extraction and GRU iterations. Specifically, the monocular depth map is fused with the RGB image to sharpen depth boundary perception and suppress matching ambiguities. The fused image is then used for feature extraction, allowing the contextual features to encode global geometric information. Furthermore, the monocular depth gradient feature is employed to guide disparity updates, helping to escape local oscillations. Finally, to address the boundary blurring of supervised disparity caused by data augmentation, we propose an edge confidence estimation method and an edge-aware loss function. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on multiple standard benchmarks, demonstrating excellent generalization while improving accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/linliboabc-maker/stereo-matching-digital.

</details>


### [43] [TextileNet: Towards Zero-shot Text-style Segmentation of Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09299)
*Anguelos Nicolaou,Antonella Ambrosio,Desiree Di Donato,Georg Vogeler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TextileNet，一种仅用合成数据训练的全卷积多任务网络，可零样本迁移至历史手稿分析，并首次通过设计包含80道问题的古文字视觉测验建立人类基准，实验表明在手写性别识别问题上需谨慎对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动作者识别系统在近期取得了显著进展，但在档案古文字学中的部署仍受限于标注数据稀缺、开放抄写者集和图像质量退化等问题。

Method: 提出TextileNet，一种全卷积多任务网络，仅使用合成数据训练以生成密集的像素级纹理嵌入，并零样本迁移至历史手稿分析。同时设计了一个包含80组配对与三元组问题的古文字视觉测验，用于建立人类基线。

Result: TextileNet在零样本检索任务中表现出色，实验结果表明该网络在古文字领域具备可信度，同时揭示了手写性别识别问题需要谨慎对待。

Conclusion: TextileNet在古文字学领域具有应用潜力，但手写中的性别判别问题在实验层面仍需谨慎处理。

Abstract: Automatic writer identification systems have progressed remarkably in recent years, yet their deployment in archival paleography remains limited by the scarcity of labeled training data, open scribe sets, and degraded image quality. We present TextileNet, a fully convolutional multi-task network trained exclusively on synthetic data to produce dense pixel-level texture embeddings, which we transfer zeroshot to historical manuscript analysis. As an original contribution to evaluation methodology, we designed a paleographic visual quiz of 80 pair and triplet questions and administered it to a range from lay participants to senior paleographers under strict anonymity, establishing to our knowledge for the first time a human baseline for script-style discrimination on late medieval text. We employ TextileNet embeddings to perform zero-shot retrieval on sub-word granularity for hand and gender identification. Our experimental results help in building the credibility of TextileNet in the paleographic domain, but more than that demonstrate in experimental terms that the question of gender in handwriting needs to be treated with caution.

</details>


### [44] [From Classification to Localization and Clinical Validation: Large-Scale Development of a Deep Learning System for Thoracic Disease Detection on Chest Radiographs in Thailand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09305)
*Isarun Chamveha,Tretap Promwiset,Napat Wanchaitanawong,Trongtum Tongdee,Pairash Saiviroonporn,Warasinee Chaisangmongkon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Inspectra CXR version 5，一个基于DenseNet-121、ACM模块和PCAM聚合层的深度学习系统，用于胸片多标签疾病分类和弱监督病变定位。模型在泰国Siriraj医院87万张胸片上训练，在域内测试集上平均AUROC达0.994，在泰国13家医院泛化集上平均AUROC为0.970，定位LLF达77.9%，放射科医生SUS评分89，表明本地化开发的CXR系统可达到高精度并跨中心泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰国及东南亚地区放射科医生严重短缺，本地化适配的深度学习胸片分析模型已被证明能显著提升对泰国人群的诊断准确性，因此需要开发一个高精度、可泛化且受放射科医生信任的胸片分析系统。

Method: 采用DenseNet-121作为骨干网络，耦合Attend-and-Compare Modules (ACM)和Probabilistic Class Activation Map (PCAM)聚合层，实现多标签胸部疾病分类与弱监督病变定位。使用Siriraj医院874,858张正面胸片及放射科报告进行训练。

Result: 域内测试集（19,871例）平均AUROC 0.994（敏感性92.4%，特异性98.6%）；跨13家医院泛化集（5,992例）平均AUROC 0.970；定位评估（4,549例）平均LLF 77.9%；放射科医生评估分类一致率93.6%，定位一致率94.7%，SUS评分89。

Conclusion: 本地开发的具有定位能力的胸片分析系统可达到高诊断精度，在泰国不同医院间具有良好的泛化能力，并获得了放射科医生的信任。

Abstract: Chest radiography (CXR) remains the most widely used thoracic imaging modality, yet expert interpretation is constrained by a severe shortage of radiologists in Thailand and across Southeast Asia. Local adaptation of deep learning models to Thai data has been shown to substantially improve accuracy on Thai populations. Here we present the development and comprehensive validation of the chest radiograph analysis model in Inspectra CXR version 5, a deep learning system that performs multi-label thoracic disease classification and weakly supervised lesion localization within a single model. The architecture couples a DenseNet-121 backbone with Attend-and-Compare Modules (ACM) and a Probabilistic Class Activation Map (PCAM) aggregation layer, producing a per-condition classification score and heatmap simultaneously. The model was developed on 874,858 frontal chest radiographs with paired radiologist reports from Siriraj Hospital, Bangkok. On a held-out, radiologist-verified in-domain test set of 19,871 cases, it achieved a mean AUROC of 0.994 (mean sensitivity 92.4%, specificity 98.6%) across nine clinically important conditions. On an independent generalization set of 5,992 cases from 13 hospitals across Thailand, the mean AUROC was 0.970, indicating robust transfer across sites. For localization, evaluated on 4,549 radiologist-annotated cases, the model attained a mean lesion-localization fraction (LLF) of 77.9% at 0.59 non-lesion localizations per image. In a usability evaluation with five thoracic radiologists, the system reached a classification concordance of 93.6%, a localization concordance of 94.7%, and a mean System Usability Scale (SUS) score of 89. These results indicate that a locally developed, localization-capable CXR system can deliver high accuracy, generalize across heterogeneous Thai hospitals, and earn the trust of practicing radiologists.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Decomposing outgoing surface radiance into material and illumination during inverse rendering is essential for applications such as relighting and augmented reality, yet it is severely ill-posed since multiple combinations can result in the same observed colour. Capturing an object under multiple lighting conditions usually helps resolve this ambiguity as it constrains the optimization towards correct solutions. In this work, we explore the potential of reconstructing rigidly moving objects -- which provides observations of diverse light-surface interactions -- to resolve the material-lighting ambiguity in inverse rendering. For this purpose, we introduce a relightable approach that marries object tracking and reconstruction with inverse rendering for general rigidly moving objects. Our experimental analysis on synthetic data demonstrates that motion can be an advantage for disentangling material and lighting: the reconstructed material is significantly more accurate when the object is observed under rigid motion than when it is static. Moreover, results on RGB videos of real hand-held objects show that our pipeline preserves this advantage even under noisy real-world conditions.

</details>


### [46] [Simon-SR: Spatially Adaptive Modulation and Visual Prompt Adaptation for Text-Reinforced Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09351)
*Haotong Cheng,Yuxuan Li,Zijie Cui,Rongling Tan,Chenyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Simon-SR是一种基于可学习提示的多模态图像超分辨率框架，通过对比提示学习和提示引导的空间自适应细化，在无需昂贵标注的情况下实现了更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态的超分辨率方法对错误先验敏感且需要昂贵的标注，因此需要一种更鲁棒、更高效的语义挖掘和文本-图像融合方法。

Method: 提出Simon-SR框架，结合对比提示学习（Contrastive Prompt Learning）和提示引导的空间自适应细化（Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement），利用可学习提示实现高效语义挖掘和鲁棒的文本-图像融合。

Conclusion: Simon-SR通过可学习提示有效解决了多模态超分辨率中对错误先验敏感和标注成本高的问题，显著提升了超分辨率性能。

Abstract: Single Image Super-Resolution (SISR) reconstructs high-quality images from low-resolution inputs. While recent multi-modal methods improve perceptual quality, they remain sensitive to erroneous priors and require expensive annotations. To address these issues, we propose Simon-SR, a multi-modal SISR framework leveraging learnable prompts for efficient semantic mining and robust text-image fusion. Our approach combines Contrastive Prompt Learning with Prompt-Guided Spatially Adaptive Refinement to enhance multi-modal alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Simon-SR surpasses state-of-the-art methods, achieving maximum improvements of 0.50 dB in PSNR, 0.0133 in SSIM, and 0.0695 in LPIPS. Code will be released.

### [47] [CtrlVTON: Controllable Virtual Try-On via Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09362)
*Seungyong Lee,Hyun Jun Jang,Sangoh Kim,Sungjoon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIP-SAM用于实例级服装分割，以及CtrlVTON框架通过分割掩码实现对服装穿着方式（尺寸、风格、空间位置）的精确控制，在各自任务上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟试穿系统在服装转移上表现良好，但用户无法控制服装的穿着方式（如松紧、塞入/外放、开合状态及身体上的空间位置），缺乏细粒度的可控性。

Method: 1) VIP-SAM：针对特定服装实例的分割模型，解决实例级（而非类别级）分割任务；2) CtrlVTON：将试穿重构为图像编辑问题，引入分割掩码作为像素级控制信号，实现对服装布局（风格、尺寸、空间位置）的精细操控。

Result: VIP-SAM和CtrlVTON分别在各自任务上达到SOTA。CtrlVTON生成的图像在遵循用户提供布局方面显著优于强大的商业编辑系统，同时保持服装保真度相当。

Conclusion: 通过实例级分割和像素级布局控制，有效解决了虚拟试穿中用户对服装穿着方式缺乏控制的问题，为可控虚拟试穿提供了新范式。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTO) has made significant progress in realistically transferring garments onto a target person. Yet most systems give the user little control over how a garment should be worn -- its size (loose or fitted), style (e.g., tucked in or untucked, open or closed), and spatial placement on the body. We address this gap with two complementary contributions. First, we define and solve Visual-Instance-Prompt Segmentation via VIP-SAM: given a flatlay image of a garment, segment that specific instance in a photograph of a person wearing it. This is an instance-level task, distinct from the typically studied category-level segmentation. Second, we introduce CtrlVTON, a controllable VTO framework that recasts try-on as an image editing problem and adds segmentation masks as pixel-level control over garment layout, including style, size, and spatial placement on the body. VIP-SAM and CtrlVTON each achieve state-of-the-art results on their respective tasks. In particular, CtrlVTON generates images that follow user-provided layouts far more faithfully than the strongest proprietary editing systems while matching them on garment fidelity.

</details>


### [48] [SVF-CR: Synchronized Visual-Facial Cross-Refinement for Multimodal Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09417)
*Hyein Park,Namho Kim,Junhwa Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种同步视觉-面部交叉精炼框架（SVF-CR），通过多模态证据融合来识别矛盾心理和犹豫行为，在BAH公开数据集上取得了0.7156的macro-F1成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾心理和犹豫是微妙的行为状态，需要结合言语、面部、视觉和声学等多种模态信息进行识别，现有方法在跨模态时序对齐与交互建模方面存在不足。

Method: 提出SVF-CR框架：1）对视频段和裁剪面部段进行同步时序分割；2）通过模态内自注意力和双向视觉-面部交叉注意力实现特征精炼；3）构建段级视觉-面部一致性与差异性证据；4）通过时序自注意力和注意力池化整合证据；5）在决策阶段与文本、声学特征进行成对证据融合。

Result: 在BAH公开评估集上，SVF-CR取得0.7156的macro-F1，优于全局视觉-面部令牌融合和同步证据基线方法。

Conclusion: 同步视觉-面部交叉精炼方法能有效捕捉多模态时序对齐的行为证据，提升矛盾心理与犹豫行为识别的性能。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy are subtle behavioral states that are expressed through a combination of verbal content, facial behavior, visual context, and acoustic cues. Effective recognition therefore requires not only extracting informative unimodal representations, but also modeling how temporally aligned behavioral evidence interacts across modalities. In this paper, we propose a synchronized visual-facial cross-refinement framework (SVF-CR) with pairwise multimodal evidence fusion for ambivalence and hesitancy recognition. The proposed method first extracts whole-video segment tokens and cropped-face segment tokens using the same temporal partition. The synchronized visual and facial tokens are refined through intra-modal self-attention and bidirectional visual-facial cross-attention, allowing whole-video context and local facial behavior to mutually refine each other before evidence construction. We then construct segment-level visual-facial evidence using consistency and discrepancy modeling, followed by temporal self-attention and attention pooling. Textual and acoustic features are lightly refined through context self-attention and are fused with the enhanced visual-facial evidence at the final decision stage using pairwise evidence fusion. Experiments on the BAH (Behavioral Ambivalence/Hesitancy) public evaluation split show that the proposed synchronized visual-facial cross-refinement improves public macro-F1 over both global visual-face token fusion and synchronized evidence baselines, achieving a public macro-F1 of 0.7156. Code is available at : https://github.com/hiinnnii/BAH-Challenge-ECCV2026\_SVF-CR.

</details>


### [49] [Multimodal Scenario Similarity Search for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09428)
*Tamás Matuszka,András Tamásy,Balázs Szolár*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合视觉和轨迹表示的多模态框架用于自动驾驶场景检索，实验表明两类特征互补，融合后效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶场景检索方法要么依赖视觉表示，要么依赖运动描述，难以理解各自的优缺点，因此需要统一框架进行对比分析并探索多模态融合的有效性。

Method: 构建多模态检索框架，包含两种轨迹方法（显式匹配的Exo-Trajectory和基于对比学习的Transformer模型ScenarioFormer），与强视觉基线对比，并融合视觉与轨迹信息进行检索。

Result: 轨迹表示在运动相关场景（如切入、转弯、排队）中表现优异；视觉嵌入在外观信息丰富时更优；两者融合始终提升检索质量，取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 外观与运动捕获是互补的场景相似性度量，多模态检索系统对自动驾驶数据挖掘、数据集整理和场景验证具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large-scale autonomous-driving datasets contain vast numbers of recorded scenarios, creating a need for efficient retrieval methods that can identify situations similar to a given query. Existing approaches typically rely on either visual representations or motion-based descriptions, making it difficult to understand their relative strengths and limitations for scenario retrieval. In this work, we present a multimodal framework for autonomous-driving scenario retrieval that combines visual and trajectory-based representations within a unified retrieval pipeline. We investigate two trajectory-based approaches: Exo-Trajectory, an explicit matching method based on surrounding-agent motion, and ScenarioFormer, a transformer-based representation learned from object trajectories using contrastive learning. We compare these approaches against strong vision-based baselines and analyze their behavior across a diverse set of driving scenarios. Experimental results show that trajectory representations provide strong retrieval performance for motion-centric events such as cut-ins, turning maneuvers, and traffic queueing, while visual embeddings excel when appearance cues are informative. Most importantly, combining visual and trajectory information consistently improves retrieval quality, yielding the best overall performance. These findings demonstrate that appearance and motion capture are complementary notions of scenario similarity and motivate multimodal retrieval systems for autonomous-driving data mining, dataset curation, and scenario-based validation.

</details>


### [50] [Parameter-Efficient Vision-Language Adaptation with Continuous Metadata Conditioning for Animal Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09443)
*Anil Osman Tur,Tonje Knutsen Sordalen,Kim Tallaksen Halvorsen,Cigdem Beyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数高效的CLIP适配框架用于动物再识别，核心创新是连续元数据条件化机制，将数值属性直接融入提示表示，在保持纯视觉推理的同时提升对长期外观变化和时间分布偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期动物再识别需要应对逐渐的形态演变和季节性的外观变化，现有的视觉-语言模型虽然提供强大的预训练表示，但在纵向生态场景中面临身份和时间分布偏移的挑战。

Method: 提出参数高效的CLIP适配框架，包含低秩视觉适配、基于提示的监督和跨模态对齐；核心创新是连续元数据条件化机制，将数值属性直接融入提示表示，而非离散化为文本类别。

Result: 在七年纵向鱼类数据集和多个野生动物基准上，在闭集、开集和时间感知评估协议下均取得改进性能。

Conclusion: 连续元数据条件化提升了对长期外观变化和时间分布偏移的鲁棒性，参数高效适配使测试时无需元数据即可进行纯视觉推理。

Abstract: Long-term animal re-identification (ReID) must remain robust to gradual morphological evolution and seasonal appearance shifts. Although recent vision-language models provide strong pretrained visual representations, adapting them to longitudinal ecological settings remains challenging, particularly under identity and temporal distribution shifts. We present a parameter-efficient CLIP adaptation framework for animal ReID and introduce a continuous metadata-conditioning mechanism that incorporates numerical attributes directly into the prompt representation during training. While low-rank visual adaptation, prompt-based supervision, and cross-modal alignment provide the adaptation framework, the proposed metadata-conditioning strategy constitutes the primary methodological contribution. By preserving the continuous structure of numerical metadata rather than discretizing it into textual categories, the proposed approach enables smooth modulation of the embedding space during training while maintaining a purely visual inference pipeline. Experiments on a seven-year longitudinal fish dataset and multiple wildlife benchmarks demonstrate improved performance under closed-set, open-set, and time-aware evaluation protocols. The results demonstrate that continuous metadata conditioning improves robustness to longitudinal appearance variation and temporal distribution shifts, while parameter-efficient adaptation enables a purely visual inference pipeline without requiring metadata at test time. Code and evaluation splits can be found at: https://github.com/AnilOsmanTur/MetaPrompt-ReID.

</details>


### [51] [Robustifying Vision-Language Models via Test-Time Prompt Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09450)
*Xingyu Zhu,Huanshen Wu,Shuo Wang,Beier Zhu,Jiannan Ge,Jiaheng Zhang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RITA框架，通过最优传输实现分布级对齐，并引入动态缓存来增强测试时对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应方法依赖样本级置信度启发式，无法区分对抗性误判与真实语义一致性，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: RITA框架：1）利用最优传输将增强视图的视觉特征分布与文本原型对齐，减轻对抗异常值；2）引入动态缓存逐步积累测试流中的可靠线索进行在线优化。

Result: 显著提升对抗鲁棒性，同时不损害干净数据的准确率。

Conclusion: 从样本级估计转向分布级对齐能有效提升VLM在对抗扰动下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP achieve strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance degrades sharply under adversarial perturbations. Existing test-time adaptation methods typically rely on sample-level confidence heuristics, overlooking the intrinsic distributional structure of the data. This sample-centric approach limits robustness, as it fails to distinguish confident adversarial mispredictions from true semantic consistency. In this work, we observe that adversarial distortion is structurally brittle: while holistic representations are corrupted, semantic integrity is often preserved in the distribution of augmented views. Motivated by this insight, we propose RITA, a Robust test-tIme prompt-TAdaptation framework that shifts from sample-level estimates to distribution-level alignment. Specifically, RITA employs optimal transport to align the distribution of augmented visual features with textual prototypes, mitigating adversarial outliers and rectifying cross-modal semantic misalignment. Furthermore, we introduce a dynamic cache to progressively accumulate reliable cues from the test stream for online refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RITA significantly improves adversarial robustness without compromising clean accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Hydra++: Real-Time Hierarchical 3D Scene Graph Construction With Object-Level Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09455)
*Hyungtae Lim,Nathan Hughes,Xihang Yu,Ruihan Xu,Yun Chang,Jingnan Shi,Rajat Talak,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hydra++是一个系统级框架，将学习型形状估计器集成到分层3D场景图中，通过类别无关形状估计、重投影一致性检查以及混合LiDAR-相机配置，改善对象和场景级别的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图系统对物体几何建模粗糙，依赖部分点云或类级CAD模板，缺乏实例级细节。Hydra++旨在通过集成学习型形状估计器，实现更精细的实例级形状重建。

Method: Hydra++整合了类别无关形状估计和重投影-掩码一致性检查；基于CRISP配置实现在线场景图构建；支持SAM3D等模块替代方案以权衡泛化与延迟；并采用混合LiDAR-相机配置应对室外稀疏噪声深度测量。

Result: 在仿真和真实室外校园场景的实验中，Hydra++在对象级和场景级重建质量上均取得了提升。

Conclusion: Hydra++通过整合学习型形状估计器与多传感器融合策略，有效提升了3D场景图中物体几何的精细度和场景重建质量，展现出在复杂环境中的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs provide a hierarchical abstraction of environments by encoding spatial entities, such as objects and places, and their relationships. However, existing scene graph systems model object geometry coarsely, relying on partial point clouds or class-level CAD templates, which limits instance-specific shape detail. This paper presents Hydra++, a system-level investigation into how learning-based object shape estimators can be integrated into a hierarchical 3D scene graph pipeline. Hydra++ incorporates category-agnostic shape estimation and a reprojection-mask consistency check to reject degenerate predictions from partial observations or imprecise segmentation. In its default CRISP-based configuration, Hydra++ performs online scene graph construction; slower estimators such as SAM3D are evaluated as modular alternatives to demonstrate generalization-latency trade-offs. Furthermore, to address the challenges of sparse and noisy depth measurements in outdoor environments, Hydra++ supports a hybrid LiDAR-camera configuration for large-scale operation, improving scene-level reconstruction quality. Experiments in both simulation and real-world outdoor campus scenarios demonstrate that Hydra++ improves object- and scene-level reconstruction quality. Project page is available at https://hydra-plusplus.github.io/.

</details>


### [53] [Foveation-Guided Dynamic Token Selection for Robust and Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09480)
*Ibrahim Batuhan Akkaya,Kishaan Jeeveswaran,Bahram Zonooz,Elahe Arani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受人类视觉系统启发的Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT)，通过注视点和中央凹采样机制实现动态令牌选择，在减少计算量的同时提升了模型对噪声和对抗攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类视觉系统高效感知机制的启发，旨在将中央凹采样和眼动机制整合到视觉Transformer中，以实现自适应计算和更强的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出FDT架构，包含两个核心模块：注视模块（fixation module）用于识别注视点并过滤无关信息，中央凹模块（foveation module）用于生成具有多尺度信息的中央凹嵌入表示。

Result: 在50%注视预算设置下，FDT准确率（81.9%）超过DeiT-S（80.9%），同时降低34.57%的乘加运算量，并展现出对多种噪声和对抗攻击的固有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FDT作为一种受HVS启发的架构，在自适应计算与鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，为构建更高效、更鲁棒的人工神经网络提供了新思路。

Abstract: The human visual system (HVS) employs foveated sampling and eye movements to achieve efficient perception, conserving both metabolic energy and computational resources. Drawing inspiration from this robustness and adaptability, we introduce the Foveated Dynamic Transformer (FDT), a foveation-guided dynamic token-selection architecture that integrates these mechanisms into a vision transformer framework. The FDT exhibits strong resilience to various types of noise and adversarial attacks, despite not being explicitly trained for such challenges. This inherent robustness is achieved through the use of fixation and foveation modules: the fixation module identifies fixation points to filter out irrelevant information, while the foveation module generates foveated embeddings with multi-scale information. At the 50% fixation-budget setting, FDT achieves higher accuracy than DeiT-S (81.9% vs. 80.9%) while reducing multiply-accumulate operations by 34.57%, highlighting one operating point on its accuracy-efficiency trade-off. These attributes position FDT as an HVS-inspired step toward artificial neural networks that combine adaptive computation with improved resilience.

</details>


### [54] [Decoupling Language Guidance from Backbones for Text-Guided Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09481)
*Yungeng Liu,Xuanzi Fang,Haijin Zeng,Qi Dai,Yongyong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BTHA是一个可迁移到不同视觉和文本骨干网络的层级适配器框架，通过特征级接口注入语义指导，在多个数据集上提升了文本引导的医学图像分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的医学图像分割模型将跨模态融合、监督和解码器设计紧密耦合在特定任务架构中，导致语言模块难以跨不同视觉和文本骨干网络复用，更换编码器对时往往需要重新设计网络。

Method: 提出BTHA框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 特征级接口，通过保持形状的适配器注入语义指导；2) 层级粗到细监督策略，分解为全局图文对齐、多尺度辅助定位和边界感知掩膜细化；3) 尺度自适应门控语义引导适配器，利用分辨率特定门控控制文本注入，通道重校准抑制冗余跨模态响应。

Result: 在不同视觉骨干网络（卷积、Transformer）和文本编码器上均有效，在四个公开数据集上优于强基线方法，且计算开销适中。

Conclusion: BTHA通过解耦跨模态融合与骨干网络设计，实现了文本引导分割模块的骨干可迁移性，为医学图像分割提供了一种灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-guided medical image segmentation leverages clinical semantics to improve lesion delineation, yet many existing models bind cross-modal fusion, supervision, and decoder design into a task-specific architecture. Such tight coupling makes it difficult to reuse language guidance modules across heterogeneous vision and text backbones, and often requires redesigning the network when the encoder pair changes. This paper presents BTHA, a backbone-transferable hierarchical adapter framework for text-guided medical image segmentation. BTHA is built around a stable feature-level interface: given multi-scale visual features and a text representation, it injects semantic guidance through shape-preserving adapters while maintaining the decoder-side tensor contract. To make this interface effective, we introduce a Hierarchical Coarse-to-Fine Supervision Strategy that decomposes learning into global image-text alignment, multi-scale auxiliary localization, and boundary-aware final mask refinement. We further design a Scale-Adaptive Gated Semantic Guidance (SAGSG) adapter, where resolution-specific gates adaptively control textual injection and channel recalibration suppresses redundant cross-modal responses. Evaluations across diverse vision and text backbones show that the same adapter and supervision design remains effective across convolutional and transformer-based visual encoders as well as different language encoders. Experiments on four public datasets further demonstrate that BTHA improves strong text-guided baselines with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [55] [SigLIP-HD by Fine-to-Coarse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09488)
*Lihe Yang,Zhen Zhao,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SigLIP-HD，通过细到粗的监督设计，在不增加推理成本（使用标准分辨率输入）的情况下提升视觉表示质量，在MLLM基准测试中表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高分辨率图像带来计算和设计复杂度增加的背景下，作者探究是否能在不采用更大图像的情况下，充分挖掘模型在标准分辨率下的感知能力，实现低成本下的细粒度视觉感知。

Method: 提出一种简化的细到粗（fine-to-coarse）监督设计：让中分辨率图像的粗粒度特征去模仿高分辨率图像的细粒度特征，基于SigLIP 2模型构建框架。

Result: 在相同推理预算下生成更优的视觉token，在多个MLLM基准上持续优于基线模型，尤其在OCR相关任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的特征模仿机制，可以在不增加推理成本的前提下提升视觉感知质量，为多模态大模型的高效视觉表征提供了新思路。

Abstract: High-quality visual representation is a long-standing pursuit in computer vision. In the context of multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), feeding higher-resolution images can produce more fine-grained visual tokens. However, it introduces additional computational and design complexity, due to multiple forward passes and post-processing of increased tokens. Before simply adopting a higher resolution, have we truly unlocked the model's full perception capability at a standard resolution? Therefore, we study an interesting problem: how to achieve fine visual perception under lower cost without larger images. We present SigLIP-HD in this work. The core is a highly simple fine-to-coarse supervision design. We enforce the coarse feature of a mid-resolution image to mimic the fine-grained feature of its high-resolution version.
